**Status:** PROPOSED (L0 contracts reconciler). This is the single execution queue for lanes A–F. **No owner approval is recorded here**; every gate keeps its state in [owner-decisions](../../capstone-report/owner-decisions-2026-09-27.md).
**Refresh Trigger:** any item below changing status; any merge of #95, #97, #99, #82, #84, #74, #72, #94 or #68; any owner answer to a gate listed here; any edit to [contracts.md](contracts.md).
**Base measured:** `origin/main` @ `3b2236c` (2026-10-15); collected slot 1574.

Rules: [contracts.md](contracts.md) §b (shared files), §c (migrations), §d (count slots). Path shorthand: `be/` = `src/backend/`, `fe/` = `src/frontend/src/`; "slots" = the `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENT.md` collected-count lines, which are exempt from the no-shared-file rule (§b rule 3).

**Scheduling rules**

1. **Code slots:** at most 12 code items run at once (box-wide, all lanes; `build-wave` default `max`; owner cap 30 agents, 2026-10-16). Every pytest run goes through `scripts/pyslot.sh` (3 machine-wide slots on the 22 GiB WSL box). Lane docs that say "at most 2 code L1s" are superseded by this rule.
2. **No shared files:** two items that run at the same time never share a file (slots, `docs/INDEX.md` and `docs/_link_graph.json` excepted).
3. **Waves are dependency layers**, not dates. An item may start in an earlier wave if its dependencies are merged, its execution gates are answered, a slot is free and rules 1–2 hold.
4. **No-slot items** (docs, owner merges, PR landings with no pytest) run beside code items, subject to rule 2.
5. Humans merge, in any order that respects real code dependencies. Slots and the index are updated by L0's post-merge batch (contracts §d, SLOT-RULE-2).

**Won't plan (2026-10-19):**
- **DL-EMB-RACE** (#196 Codex finding 2). The races need a second local actor that creates an empty target directory, or deletes and recreates `.partial`, during a developer-run fetch on a single-user box; such an actor can already replace the model files directly. The dangling-symlink half does not hold on Linux (errno 20, measured by the #196 L1), and W-8-HARDEN's Design 3 chose exclusive `mkdir` ownership on purpose (HC-EMB-004c/d/e). Reopen if the fetch ever runs unattended or shared.
- ~~MSET-EXT-NOKEY-MINORS~~: **moved to a row** (MSET-EXT-NOKEY-PIN) after PR #202 review round 1. The first pass said minor 1 did not reproduce, but its scratch mutant had dropped the test's reference to `row`. With `row` held, as HC-AUDCMS-005 does, a raw-SQL key wipe in request 2 passes every assertion (measured on `5750a97`).
- **Folded, not separate rows:** EMB-HARDEN-MINORS and REPROCESS-007C-REACH are tasks of EMB-LOAD-LOG-PATH. The "LLM model paths at INFO" part of EMB-LOAD-LOG-PATH is its own row, LLM-PATH-LOG, because its files are held by #185 and B-5.

## Planned 2026-10-22, second batch

Planned 2026-10-22 on `7ffc55f` after owner prompt 32 (L0 AskUserQuestion; L0 records the answers in its own owner-rows PR **#286**, which lands first or alongside this plan PR; no owner answer is recorded here). **CACHE-STALE answered (c) "Add all three to the key"** (not the recommended (a)): row AGENT-CACHE-CARETASKS above is re-planned for (c) ([plan delta](lane-A-rag-assistant.md#plan-delta-2026-10-22-second-batch-owner-chose-c-add-all-three-to-the-key)): five new fingerprint parts (care tasks, verified medication-change entities, all observations, timeline documents, medications), measured outside today's key with a scratch script on `7ffc55f`. Medication-change entities have no `updated_at`, so that part (and the timeline documents part) hashes exactly the columns the tool reads; on today's code no medication-change edit is missed (the only in-place edit is the verification flag), which is narrower than the option text, and L0 tells the owner so. No schema migration. **Also found:** `check_verification` reads unverified observations (`modules/agent/tools/check_verification.py:72-77`), a fourth source outside the key; the "all observations" part covers it. **KV-CACHE-NEXT answered "Measure first (Recommended)":** two new rows. A `chat_context_size` default change is never part of OLLAMA-NUM-CTX; if the evidence argues for one, it becomes a separate owner-question bullet (L0 ruling on #287 round 1). **Verified on this box (read-only):** Ollama 0.40.5, RTX 3080 10240 MiB, no `OLLAMA_CONTEXT_LENGTH` in the unit, and `ollama serve --help` gives the default context as "4k/32k/256k based on VRAM", so every Ollama request here is expected to run at 4096 tokens (inferred from the help text and the Ollama docs, not measured; KV-PREFIX-MEASURE step 3 confirms) while `capabilities()` reports 8192 or 262144. **Ask-first files:** none. **Open PRs checked** (`gh pr list --state open`, 2026-10-22): #210, #230, #239, #264, #283, #284, #285, #286. #264 and #239 (parked drafts) touch `be/api/assistant.py`; draft #210 (F-9) edits `docs/INDEX.md` and `docs/_link_graph.json`, which KV-PREFIX-MEASURE lists, so that row regenerates both after each `origin/main` merge; #264 and #284 (QUEUE-E6-W1) also edit `queue.md` (other rows). **CACHE-HIT-AUDIT stays a separate owner item.**

## Planned 2026-10-22, third batch (bug diagnosis)

Planned 2026-10-22 on `5f7c67c` from the bug-diagnosis pass over the GPU-box runs (`/tmp/claude-1000/gpu-1022/`, MM-4 `/tmp/claude-1000/mm4-1022/`, F-9 dry run `/tmp/claude-1000/f9-dryrun/record.md`; GPU box only). Diagnosis table with root cause, `file:line`, severity and Codex proposal paths: `/tmp/claude-1000/bug-diagnosis-1022.md`. Haiku explorers located the code; every `file:line` was re-read by the Opus planner; Codex (`gpt-5.6-sol`, `-s read-only`, scratch worktrees) proposed diffs, which the planner reviewed and corrected where noted in each lane section. **No owner approval is recorded here.** **Ask-first files (CLAUDE.md §1):** none edited by any row; AUDIT-DB-LOCKED is treated as auth/encryption-adjacent (`core/database.py` imports the SQLCipher check) and asks first. **New gates:** JUDGE-THINK, INTERP-DISC-ORDER, EXTRACT-DATE-PRIORITY, DB-LOCK-FIX (asked only after a repro). **Answered** (owner prompts 38-39, 2026-10-22, record `/tmp/claude-1000/prompts-38-39.json`; rows via L0 PR `docs/owner-rows-prompts38-39`; no answer is recorded in this file): JUDGE-THINK (a) judge only, think off, via prompt 39 "Think off now + research Laya (Recommended)" (prompt 38 got a proposal, not an option), with a separate research row JUDGE-LAYA-RESEARCH that another planner adds; INTERP-DISC-ORDER (a) check the final text; EXTRACT-DATE-PRIORITY (a) keep earliest date; INTERP-SAFETY-ENFORCE "Plan an enforce fix" (bullet below; not planned in this PR). **L0 ruling (#289 review round 1), bundled PRs:** the bug-fix agent builds (1) LLAMA-CHAT-FORMAT + LLAMA-SERIAL + LLAMA-GPU-LAYERS as **one PR** (one file, so no ordering hazard; collected delta +10), (2) EXTRACT-UNIT-MGL + EXTRACT-DOB-DATE as **one PR** (+12), (3) INTERP-GROUNDED-MD + CHAT-MODEL-BADGE + INTERP-RENDER-2 + VERIFY-DOCS-STALE as **one frontend PR** (+2 backend for CHAT-MODEL-BADGE's route test, +8 Vitest); RUNNER-EXC-CHAIN runs after AGENT-CACHE-CARETASKS (#290) merges. Each bundle cites every row id it closes, keeps each row's acceptance and RED evidence, and lists the union of the rows' files. JUDGE-THINK and INTERP-SAFETY-META are not in any bundle and are not built before the owner-rows PR merges. **Not bugs / already covered (no row):** the legacy chat faithfulness gate withholding answers in the GPU run is conservative behaviour (one withheld answer said 162 mg/dL was "within the reference range", `gpu-1022/judge-trace.log:5`); the rest is GC5-NLI-NEUTRAL, GC5-FAITH-SCORE, RULES-RANGE-VALUE and LLAMA-CITE-DETACH. Never weakened. The `interpret_safety` "Missing required disclaimers" log is not why interpret-grounded abstains (that route never calls it; it abstained on faithfulness 0.00): the log is row INTERP-SAFETY-META. `modules/model_selector.py`'s direct `Llama` inference is B-5 (W-7). **QUEUE-E6-W1:** the edit in #284 is complete; the plan's acceptance greps matched the plan's own quotes, so this PR corrects lane E §2.30's acceptance (docs only). **Open PRs checked** (2026-10-22): #230 (`.claude/mods/` only), #239 and #264 (parked drafts: `api/assistant.py`, `api/interpretations.py`, `modules/rag.py`; hold no file, contracts §b rule 2), #284 (`queue.md` row E-6 and the "later" row, lane E §2.6 / §5 / §6; this PR edits lane E §2.30's acceptance and the QUEUE-E6-W1 row, disjoint hunks). **New bullet:** INTERP-SAFETY-ENFORCE (not planned).

- **FE-A11Y-DIALOGS** (*planned 2026-10-23: covered by UI-PRIM-OVERLAYS plus the page row that owns each dialog (Settings export and consent: UI-PG-SETTINGS; dose modal: UI-PG-MEDS; preview: UI-PG-DOCUMENTS; edit value: UI-PG-REVIEW), [Planned 2026-10-23, frontend revamp](#planned-2026-10-23-frontend-revamp)*): dialogs with no Escape handler or focus trap; Settings export (`SettingsPage.tsx:889`) and consent (`:1059`) dialogs lack `role="dialog"`; use `@radix-ui/react-dialog`. AXE-E2E's `KNOWN_A11Y` entries point here.
- **IMPORT-FAILED-REASON:** a password-protected or non-PDF `.pdf` returns 201 `extraction_failed` with no reason (`api/documents.py:698-709`); also that `except` logs `{e}` (`:699`), the RUNNER-EXC-INTERP shape for PDF parser text.
- **RECOVER-ORDER** (ask-first `api/profiles.py:726-753`): a master commit failing after key re-seal and recovery-file rotation leaves neither password nor code working.
- **AUTO-LOCK-UNUSED** (ask-first, auth): `core/config.py:54` `auto_lock_timeout_minutes` has no reader.
- **TEST-RESET-ROUTE** (ask-first `api/profiles.py:476-531`): `/profiles/test/reset` is live outside production; its 500 detail echoes `{exc}`.
- **BACKUP-RESTORE-JOURNAL** (ask-first, sealed keys): `scripts/backup.py:513` manifest not atomic; `:718-723` restore leaves a hot `-journal`.
- **FALLBACK-COUNT-50**, **CHAT-SESSIONS-N1** (`api/assistant.py`; after #264/#300): capped counts reported as exact; one COUNT per session.
- **RANGE-ORDER** (review round 1): `_parse_text_line('Glucose 105 mg/dL 99-70', 1)` stores `ref_low=99, ref_high=70` (also the colon form); a reversed range should be swapped or rejected, never stored inverted. Its property test was dropped from HYPOTHESIS-EXTRACT until this lands.
- **A11Y-CRIT-LABELS** (*planned 2026-10-24: row A11Y-CRIT-LABELS in [Planned 2026-10-24, Safety Mode build](#planned-2026-10-24-safety-mode-build)*; found 2026-10-24, AXE-E2E CI run [40068287285](https://github.com/BrooklynD23/HealthCentral/actions/runs/40068287285)): critical axe on `/explain` date inputs missing `<label>` and on `/settings` a `<select>` missing `select-name`; AXE-E2E (#329) merged with these three entries in `KNOWN_A11Y` (`fixBy: 'A11Y-CRIT-LABELS'`, two `label` on `/explain`, one `select-name` on `/settings`); this row fixes them and deletes the entries (shrink-only, never add).
- **EXTRACT-LEGEND-LINES** (found 2026-10-24, EXTRACT-PAGE-MERGE review; not yet planned): legend/target lines with units on table pages (reference-range footers, "Target: …" rows) can become unverified observations; needs a plan delta and HC tests before widening the outside-table text pass.
- **DOCS-VISIT-EXC-LOG** (found 2026-10-24, #336 adjacent path; not yet planned): `be/api/documents.py:988` logs `{e}` on the LLM-assist entity pass failure (`logger.warning(f"LLM-assist entity pass failed for {doc_id}: {e}")`); same RUNNER-EXC-INTERP shape as other provider paths.
- **EXPORT-RESTORE-PURGE** (found 2026-10-24, privacy review; not yet planned): `be/api/backup.py:453` calls `close_profile_database_on_logout` before restore but does not call `purge_profile_exports`; in-memory export packets from the pre-restore session can outlive the restore until the 30-minute TTL.
- **EXPORT-ARTIFACT-PURGE** (found 2026-10-24, privacy review; not yet planned): persisted `ExportArtifact` rows (contract I-20 / row C-GC1) are not cleared on profile delete, logout, lock or restore; coordinate with EXPORT-RESTORE-PURGE and the in-memory purge from EXPORT-STORE-PURGE.
- **RAG-TIMEOUT-GROUNDED** (*planned 2026-10-27: [delta](../2026-10-22-mvp-rows-plan.md#delta-2026-10-27-to-rag-timeout-answer-the-grounded-path-rag-timeout-grounded) on row RAG-TIMEOUT-ANSWER, waits on new owner gate RAG-TIMEOUT-GROUNDED*; found 2026-10-23, #317 stopped): chat timeout path fixed by G-B5; `POST /interpretations/observations/{id}/interpret-grounded` still needs the plan delta noted on #317 (L0 comment 6374422151) before RAG-TIMEOUT-ANSWER can resume. *Done: the delta was planned 2026-10-27 and RAG-TIMEOUT-ANSWER merged as #317 (`6b3dd27`).*

## Planned 2026-10-23, frontend revamp

Planned 2026-10-23 on `15fe095` (after #310 merged) from the owner's frontend direction and prompt 44 (L0 AskUserQuestion; L0 evidence comment 6374428883 on PR #311) and prompt 45 (L0 evidence comment 6374428087 on PR #311); owner-decisions rows UI-DIRECTION, UI-SAAS-MEANING, UI-SCOPE-1031, UI-DARK-MODE, UI-SAFETY-TOGGLE, UI-DEPS, UI-FIXTURE-NAME land in the same PR as this section, merging two independent designer proposals. **Plan:** one file, [2026-10-23-frontend-revamp-plan.md](../2026-10-23-frontend-revamp-plan.md) (each row links its section); research package [docs/research/2026-10-23-frontend-revamp/](../../research/2026-10-23-frontend-revamp/00-README.md) (proposals, comparison, prototype patches, screenshots). **No owner approval is recorded here.** **Answers used:** UI-SAAS-MEANING "Craft + bold showcase"; UI-SCOPE-1031 "Full plan, no freeze" (L0 reading: every row is queued now and starts when its own dependencies merge; tokens and shell first; each page migrates completely in its row); UI-DARK-MODE "Follow OS setting" (light until UI-DARK-FLIP, which is gated on a no-unmigrated-colour test); UI-SAFETY-TOGGLE "Make it real" (research row SAFETY-MODE-REAL; until a build row lands the toggle stays, relabelled, plan §3.6); UI-DEPS "Keep framer-motion 10" (no `motion` 14; the other listed packages approved; any other major back to the owner); UI-FIXTURE-NAME "Replace with fixture" (applied in the research package). **Answered in prompt 48 (L0 evidence comment 6380025171 on PR #311):** UI-ROUTE-MOTION "Whole-page fade" (every page fades opacity .35→1, ~200 ms, values included; the plan makes it opacity-only, ≤ 240 ms, then still), UI-AUTH-SCREENS "Layout, motion and Lift" (UI-PG-AUTH and UI-PG-SECURITY no longer gated), SESSION-LOCK-NOW "Add Lock now" (SESSION-REENTRY Task 3 ready once UI-SHELL merges; Task 1 merged as #312). **New gates (open, not asked):** SAFETY-MODE-BEHAVIOUR (after SAFETY-MODE-REAL), SAFETY-MODE-DIFF (per ask-first file of the chosen behaviour); non-blocking open questions SAFETY-LABEL-WORDING, UI-NAV-NAMES, UI-BRAND-MARK, MED-BADGES, UI-ABOUT-PLACE, INK-MOMENT; drafts in the plan's [§11](../2026-10-23-frontend-revamp-plan.md#11-owner-gates-and-open-questions). **Ask-first files:** none edited by any row here (SAFETY-MODE-REAL is research only; no `UI-*` row edits a backend file). **Acceptance for every `UI-*` row:** gate G (axe 0 serious/critical in light and dark, CLS < 0.1, INP proxy < 200 ms at CPU ×4, bundle budgets, reduced-motion snapshot, no horizontal scroll at 390 and 320, zero console errors and no non-localhost request) plus G10, the stillness probe on every `[data-t0]` element (effective opacity and bounding rect; fails on zero nodes; owners of `data-t0` in plan §2.7), plus the defect checks DF-1…DF-6 its section names. **Motion rules:** every page change is the owner's opacity-only route fade (UI-ROUTE-MOTION "Whole-page fade", ≤ 240 ms, then still; under reduced motion instant); overlays holding T0 content open with M0 (L0 reading). **e2e contract changes:** every `src/frontend/e2e/*.spec.ts` assertion a row changes is listed old → new in plan [Appendix A](../2026-10-23-frontend-revamp-plan.md#appendix-a-e2e-assertions-and-the-row-that-changes-each) and in that row's files ([plan §6](../2026-10-23-frontend-revamp-plan.md#6-standard-acceptance-gate-and-defect-checks)). **Shared-file rules:** `src/frontend/package.json` only in UI-MOTION, UI-BUDGET, UI-CHART-CLEANUP (never beside AXE-E2E or another `package.json` PR); `fe/App.tsx` only in UI-ROUTES, UI-PG-RESULTS, UI-OVERVIEW, UI-ABOUT (different waves); `fe/components/ui/index.ts` only in UI-PRIM-CONTROLS. **Overlaps with MVP rows** (plan [§9](../2026-10-23-frontend-revamp-plan.md#9-reconciliation-with-rows-already-on-main)): UI-MOTION is never beside SESSION-REENTRY Task 1, FE-IMPORT-ERROR, IMPORT-SCANNED-PAGE, FE-VERIFY-NUMERIC, FE-EXPLAIN-ERR or BADGE-TICK (L0 order: those first); page rows depend on the MVP row that owns their page. **Rows planned: 31** (30 `UI-*` + SAFETY-MODE-REAL) plus SESSION-REENTRY Task 3 (a delta to the existing row, not a new row). **MVP priority** as in the MVP section (P0 safety/privacy/lab-value claim false or phone unusable; P1 demo path; P2 after).

**Ready to build now:** none from this block (GATES-SAMPLE-TIME done, #374); UI-SHELL is in flight (#370); the 2026-10-26 follow-up rows are in [Planned 2026-10-26, post-merge batch](#planned-2026-10-26-post-merge-batch); the SAFETY-MODE build rows are planned in [Planned 2026-10-24, Safety Mode build](#planned-2026-10-24-safety-mode-build) (SAFETY-MODE-REAL done, #338). **Next:** UI-GATES (#350), UI-MOTION (#351), UI-TOKENS (#356), UI-ROUTES (#357), UI-BUDGET (#361), UI-PRIM-OVERLAYS (#363), UI-PRIM-FEEDBACK (#364) and UI-PRIM-CONTROLS (#365) merged; UI-RIBBON (#362), UI-PROVENANCE (#367) and UI-SHOWCASE-KIT (#368) merged; UI-SHELL in flight (#370). UI-MOTION brought the main chunk to 157.52 kB gz (budget 180), and UI-ROUTES measures 103.76 kB gz on its branch; L0 runs the MVP rows that share UI-MOTION's files first. **Gates L0 can ask now:** none (UI-AUTH-SCREENS and SESSION-LOCK-NOW answered in prompt 48). **Delta to SESSION-REENTRY** (row above in the MVP section): Task 3 "Lock now" ([plan §8.2](../2026-10-23-frontend-revamp-plan.md#82-delta-to-session-reentry-task-3-lock-now)). **GAUGE-ZERO-WIDTH** merged (#319); UI-PG-RESULTS keeps its HC-GZW cases green.

**New rows (not yet planned)** (found while planning, 2026-10-23):
- **VERIFY-UNDO:** the verify action cannot be undone in the UI: `be/api/observations.py:384` has `POST /{observation_id}/verify` and no reverse route. Both designers drew an Undo on verify; the plan ships none. A backend row (audit-logged, per-profile, `route_client` tests) if the owner wants it.
- **ICON-RAIL-TABLET:** proposal B's 768–1023 px icon rail is not built (one breakpoint at 1024 instead; comparison #18).
- **RECOVER-CACHE-CLEAR** (found 2026-10-23, #312 security review and PR body; not yet planned): `src/frontend/src/pages/RecoverProfile.tsx:73` navigates to /inbox without `queryClient.clear()`, the same cross-profile cache gap #312 fixed for the unlock path.
- **RECOVERY-CODE-RATELIMIT** (found 2026-10-23, #321 PR body; not yet planned): `POST /profiles/{id}/recovery-code` has no rate limiter. Auth code: ask-first.
- **KV-NUMCTX-VERIFY** (*planned 2026-10-27: row KV-NUMCTX-VERIFY in [Planned 2026-10-27, planner](#planned-2026-10-27-planner)*; found 2026-10-23, #318 PR body): a model-host re-run of KV-PREFIX-MEASURE step 3 to show Ollama uses `num_ctx` 8192 after OLLAMA-NUM-CTX.
- **CPU-DEFAULT-PIN** (found 2026-10-23, #315 review MINOR; not yet planned; optional): an extra HC-CPU-002 assert, `(16.0, 100.0, 8.0) == "gemma4-12b"`.
- **SRE-002-WEAK** (found 2026-10-23, #312 PR body; not yet planned): HC-SRE-002 passes without `gcTime: 0` because `clear()` empties the cache; only HC-SRE-003 guards the edit.
- **SAFETY-MODE-DUPROW** (found 2026-10-24, #349 review; not yet planned): `is_strict_safety_mode` returns true if ANY `user_model_settings` row has the flag, while GET/PATCH `/assistant/safety-mode` read and write one row, so duplicate rows can show OFF while enforcing ON (stricter only). Make PATCH write every row or dedupe.
- **A11Y-LABEL-MINORS** (found 2026-10-24, #348 review; not yet planned): HC-A11YCL-003 uses a loose `/provider/i` query; the API Key and Model labels in `SettingsPage.tsx` and the Category label in `MemoryManager.tsx` have no `htmlFor`.
- **IMPORT-SCANNED-MINORS** (found 2026-10-24, #347 review; not yet planned): the five MINOR findings on PR #347: (1) `be/api/documents.py:293` comment "Null when the pipeline did not run" is also null on the `pending_ocr` early return and on extraction failure; (2) no test for the reprocess wiring (`be/api/documents.py:1223`; add one `route_client` test on `POST /documents/{id}/reprocess` asserting `pages_without_text == [2]`); (3) the join for three or more pages (`fe/pages/DocumentInbox.tsx:42`) is untested (add a `[2, 3, 4]` case to HC-ISP-004); (4) the `_text_then_image_pdf` writer is copied into `be/tests/test_documents_api.py:1276` and `be/tests/test_extraction_pipeline.py:964` (move to `tests/support/minimal_pdf.py`); (5) `be/tests/test_documents_api.py:1278` has a redundant `import io as _io` (use `io`).
- **MOTION-EXPORTS-DELETE** (found 2026-10-24, #351 L0 ruling; not yet planned): UI-MOTION keeps `PageTransition` and `modalVariants` in `fe/components/ui/Motion.tsx`; UI-SHELL (AppLayout) and the page/overlay rows that replace `DoseLoggingModal.tsx` and the `SettingsPage.tsx` modals delete them.
- **UI-GATES-TIMING-FLAKE** (found 2026-10-24, #350 L0 review; not yet planned): HC-UIG-002/003/005/006 failed under CPU contention (load ~6.7; a 180 ms sampling gap vs the 40 ms G10 bound) and passed alone; make the timing gates robust or run them serially. Also from #350: plan G10 wording should state the route-container opacity rule; `e2e/` is in no tsconfig; axe `label-content-name-mismatch` on `/inbox` has no owning row.
- **SAFETY-STRICT-CHAT-AUDIT** (found 2026-10-24, #355 review; not yet planned): the strict legacy-chat abstain writes no audit row of its own (interpret-grounded does).
- **SAFETY-VERIFIED-MINORS** (found 2026-10-24, #359 review; not yet planned): the batch refusal audit sits inside the per-item try; no `force_regenerate` refusal test; the batch test checks neither audit details nor OFF; `NEXT_DAY` constants unused (one-day panel bound unproven); the OFF-parity test covers only the two POST routes.
- **EXTRACT-DOB-MINORS** (found 2026-10-24, #358; not yet planned): a DOB in a row whose token count differs from its header is still not skipped (strict xfail pins it); only one side of the token-count check is tested; `Patient DOB` followed by a line `Received 03/04/2026` now yields no date (accepted as ambiguous by L0).
- **SHOWCASE-PROVENANCE-MOVES** (found 2026-10-25, L0 rulings on #367 and #368; not yet planned): UI-SHOWCASE-KIT keeps EmptyState `action` optional; UI-PG-CARE and UI-PG-RECORDS make it required when they migrate (call sites CareTasksPage.tsx:281, PinboardsPage.tsx:108, SearchPage.tsx:173/181/189, TimelinePage.tsx:202/210). UI-SHOWCASE-KIT's G1-G3, G5-G7, G10 and per-recipe G5 screenshots run in UI-PG-DOCUMENTS, UI-OVERVIEW and UI-ABOUT. UI-PROVENANCE's HC-UIPV-004 (DF-2 wrap at 1440/390) runs in UI-PG-REVIEW, UI-PG-RESULTS and UI-PG-ASK; HC-UIPV-005/006 (verify toast on a route) run in UI-SHELL and UI-PG-REVIEW. CitationTooltip citations on /interpret are knowledge-base refs with no observation id or page, so no page is shown for them.
- **PAGE-H1** (found 2026-10-25, L0 ruling on #370; not yet planned): the brand is no longer an h1, so routes without their own h1 show none. UI-SHELL asserts the shell renders no h1 and no route renders more than one; "exactly one h1 per route" moves to the UI-PG-* rows and UI-MICRO (the #370 PR body lists the 10 routes).
- **PAGE-A11Y** (found 2026-10-25, L0 ruling on #370; not yet planned): the serious/critical axe findings in page content on 11 of 15 routes (color-contrast, button-name, target-size) belong to the UI-PG-* rows; the #370 PR body lists them per route. No `KNOWN_A11Y` entry.
- **GATES-POPPER-TRANSFORM** (found 2026-10-25, L0 ruling on #367; not yet planned): G5 reads the Radix popper's static positioning `transform` (`matrix(1, 0, 0, 1, 223, 447)`) as motion. A UI-GATES harness row lets G5 tell a static positioning transform from a running animation, without relaxing it for real motion.
- **UI-TOKENS-BADGE-TO-SHELL** (found 2026-10-24, #356 L0 ruling; not yet planned): five `KNOWN_A11Y` TopBar "ON" badge entries (`e2e/accessibility.spec.ts:94-114`, selector `.px-1\.5`, on /inbox, /verify, /trends, /explain, /settings) now name `fixBy: 'UI-SHELL'`; UI-SHELL must delete them when it replaces the badge.
- **ALERTDIALOG-CANCEL-FOCUS** (found 2026-10-25, #363 L0 review; *planned 2026-10-26: row ALERTDIALOG-CANCEL-FOCUS, with the HC-UIOV-002 `modal={false}` gap; the Dialog close-button scroll on tall content stays here, not planned*): a destructive AlertDialog must put initial focus on Cancel, not the first focusable element. Blocks any destructive-flow consumer (DangerZone / UI-PG-SECURITY, FE-A11Y-DIALOGS). Also: the HC-UIOV-002 focus-trap test passes with `modal={false}`; the Dialog close button scrolls away on tall content.
- **TREND-POINT-RANGE** (found 2026-10-25, #362 L0 ruling; not yet planned): TrendPoint carries no per-point ref range, so RangeRibbon labels say "this chart's range". A backend row adds per-point ranges, then per-point wording.
- **VITEST-TZ-PIN** (found 2026-10-25, #364; *planned 2026-10-28: row [VITEST-TZ-PIN](#planned-2026-10-28-follow-ups), a config pin; the test checks zone-dependent behaviour by design, and Node ignores a `TZ` write inside the `threads` pool used on `/mnt`*): `Gamification.test.tsx:202` assumes US Eastern; pin `TZ` for vitest in its own chore.
- **BUTTON-DARK-CONTRAST** (found 2026-10-25, #365 L0 ruling; not yet planned): Button primary `text-white` fails dark contrast on /setup; owned by UI-DARK-FLIP.
- **MOTION-BARREL** (found 2026-10-25, #365 L0 ruling; not yet planned): stop re-exporting Motion from `fe/components/ui/index.ts` when its six importers migrate.
- **PROGRESS-CLIPPATH** (found 2026-10-25, #364 L0 ruling; not yet planned): Progress fill uses `scaleX`, which G5 reads as a transform under reduced motion; move the fill to `clip-path` (not a G5 allowlist) before UI-SHELL runs G5.
- **UI-TOKENS-OPEN** (found 2026-10-25, #356; not yet planned): HC-UITK-005 vs HC-UITK-007 id clash; G5 vs spinners; owner row for the §2.3 type scale; the HC-UIG-002 mark goes to UI-SHELL.
- **UI-PRIM-CONTROLS-MINORS** (found 2026-10-25, #365 review; not yet planned): the three MINOR findings on the final PR #365 review: (1) `IconButton` `loading` adds the spinner beside the icon (layout shift); (2) `Button` `loading` with `asChild` stays clickable; (3) Select and Checkbox have no direct test (only the transition-all scan renders them).
- **POPOVER-OPEN-COST** (found 2026-10-26, L0 ruling 08:46:34Z on #370; *planned 2026-10-26: row POPOVER-OPEN-COST*): the browser half of HC-UIOV-007 moves out of UI-SHELL; the popover open costs 49-53 ms on a `vite preview` build, all under `ui/Popover.tsx`.
- **SETTINGS-DETECT-BLOCKING** (found 2026-10-26, L0 ruling 06:35:47Z on #370; *planned 2026-10-26: row SETTINGS-DETECT-BLOCKING*): `detect_hardware` blocks the event loop in three settings routes, about 2.6 s each, serialised; E2E-SET-001 cold start takes 10.2 s on main against its 10 s limit.
- **GATES-SAMPLE-TIME-MINORS** (found 2026-10-25, #374 review round 1; *planned 2026-10-26: row GATES-SAMPLE-TIME-MINORS; MINOR 3 (the fixture uses a fulfilled `page.route`, not `page.setContent`) is recorded in the plan section, no code change*).
- **INBOX-DATA-RENDER** (found 2026-10-26, #370 L0 ruling; *folded into UI-PG-DOCUMENTS 2026-10-26 as a measurement*): the `DocumentInbox` list renders in about 48-51 ms when its data arrives (dev server, CPU profile); UI-PG-DOCUMENTS measures it on a `vite preview` build. A separate row only if that build still shows it over 40 ms.
- **E2E-SET-003-WEAK** (found 2026-10-26, L0 ruling 5 at 02:41Z on #370; *folded into UI-PG-SETTINGS 2026-10-26, which owns `e2e/settings-smoke.spec.ts`*): on main E2E-SET-003 passes on the settings description text without any dialog open, so it cannot see a missing consent dialog (D12).
- **GATES-PASS3-ELAPSED** (found 2026-10-25, #374 first run; *folded into row [UIG006-PASS3-FLAKE](#planned-2026-10-28-follow-ups) 2026-10-28, which reproduces first*): HC-UIG-006 pass-3 failed once with `elapsedMs` 1657 < 2000. The #374 review showed it cannot come from #374's diff (`elapsedMs` and the lazy-idle wait run on Node's `Date.now()` and Playwright network events); unexplained, and not to be filed as flake. Needs a reproduction before a row.
- **STILLNESS-GAP-BOUND** (found 2026-10-26, #370 rulings; *asked 2026-10-28 by L0 (AskUserQuestion, after two Opus review rounds) and answered **"Fix first, frame waits"** (owner-decisions row STILLNESS-GAP-BOUND); then amended in chat as STILLNESS-NAV-70; planned 2026-10-28: rows [GATES-NAV-BOUND and STILLNESS-NAV-LATE](#planned-2026-10-28-planner)*): the 40 ms sampling-gap bound is about one React commit on this box and in CI, and main exceeds it on `/settings` with no long task. By 2026-10-27 the misses showed on every page change and on today's app: on dev, origin/main missed in 9 of 15 runs and #370 in 8 of 15. Then, the same day in chat, the owner said: "Raise it to 70 ms for page changes and keep diagnosing" (owner-decisions row STILLNESS-NAV-70). Page changes now allow 70 ms (row GATES-NAV-BOUND, L0), and UI-SHELL (#370) waits for that row. STILLNESS-NAV-LATE keeps diagnosing at 40 ms.
- **RSI-LOG-CMD** (found 2026-10-26, rsi-loop run `2026-10-26-gpubox`; not yet planned): the run proposed nothing, because the rsi-log records no command or stderr signature. Recording a redacted command signature is privacy-relevant, so it needs owner sign-off before it is planned.
- **AUDIT-GUARD-ATTR-DYNAMIC** (found 2026-10-27, #385 L0 ruling, comment 6442124521; *planned 2026-10-28: row [AUDIT-GUARD-ATTR-DYNAMIC](#planned-2026-10-28-follow-ups)*): `be/tests/support/callgraph.py` `dynamic_sites` (`:99`) does not flag a bound attribute passed as a value, e.g. `asyncio.to_thread(selector.detect_hardware_tier)` in `list_tiers` and `get_model_settings` (`be/api/model_settings.py:598` and `:398`), so that edge disappears silently from HC-AUD-ORD-030 (`be/tests/test_audit_order_guard.py`).
- **G1-VERIFY-TRACE-RACE** (found 2026-10-28, #390 CI run 40643774878; *planned 2026-10-28: row [G1-VERIFY-TRACE-RACE](#planned-2026-10-28-follow-ups)*): `e2e/ui-provenance.spec.ts:546` "Gate G1 /verify own nodes have no serious or critical violation in light or dark" failed once and passed on retry (CI reported `1 flaky`). Error: `after the interaction: own selector matches no visible node: [data-page-image-overlay] [data-trace-mark]`. The /verify trace mark is absent after the interaction: a race between the interaction and the overlay render. Not caused by #390 (the failing assertion is in `ui-provenance.spec.ts`, which #390 does not touch); no earlier occurrence is recorded. CI E2E retries=2 hide it, so a green job does not show it.
- **UIG006-PASS3-FLAKE** (found 2026-10-28, #390 review, PR body; *planned 2026-10-28: row [UIG006-PASS3-FLAKE](#planned-2026-10-28-follow-ups), with GATES-PASS3-ELAPSED folded in*): HC-UIG-006 pass 3 flaked once on an unmutated interaction path: `elapsedMs` 741 < 2000. The lazy-route network-idle wait can finish before the delayed fetch starts. Not caused by #390 (it touches no lazy-route code); the final full run (`elapsedMs` 2625) and an earlier full run (`elapsedMs` 2627) on that branch passed.

## Planned 2026-10-24, Safety Mode build

Planned 2026-10-24 on `29dca22` from the owner's answer to SAFETY-MODE-BEHAVIOUR (prompt 51, owner-decisions row SAFETY-MODE-BEHAVIOUR): "Stricter checks", "Always-on citations view", "Verified-only data". **Plan:** [2026-10-24-safety-mode-build-plan.md](../2026-10-24-safety-mode-build-plan.md) (each row links its section); research [docs/research/2026-10-23-safety-mode-real/](../../research/2026-10-23-safety-mode-real/00-README.md). **No owner approval is recorded here.** L0 reading, not the owner's words: one per-profile switch turns all three behaviours on together; OFF is exactly today's behaviour and ON is only stricter. No row edits `faithfulness.py`, `verifier_agent.py` or `interpret_safety.py`; a build that finds it must stops for SAFETY-MODE-DIFF.
